Tool:      1	---
     2	name: chembl-database
     3	description: Query ChEMBL bioactive molecules and drug discovery data. Search compounds by structure/properties, retrieve bioactivity data (IC50, Ki), find inhibitors, perform SAR studies, for medicinal chemistry.
     4	license: Unknown
     5	metadata:
     6	    skill-author: K-Dense Inc.
     7	---
     8	
     9	# ChEMBL Database
    10	
    11	## Overview
    12	
    13	ChEMBL is a manually curated database of bioactive molecules maintained by the European Bioinformatics Institute (EBI), containing over 2 million compounds, 19 million bioactivity measurements, 13,000+ drug targets, and data on approved drugs and clinical candidates. Access and query this data programmatically using the ChEMBL Python client for drug discovery and medicinal chemistry research.
    14	
    15	## When to Use This Skill
    16	
    17	This skill should be used when:
    18	
    19	- **Compound searches**: Finding molecules by name, structure, or properties
    20	- **Target information**: Retrieving data about proteins, enzymes, or biological targets
    21	- **Bioactivity data**: Querying IC50, Ki, EC50, or other activity measurements
    22	- **Drug information**: Looking up approved drugs, mechanisms, or indications
    23	- **Structure searches**: Performing similarity or substructure searches
    24	- **Cheminformatics**: Analyzing molecular properties and drug-likeness
    25	- **Target-ligand relationships**: Exploring compound-target interactions
    26	- **Drug discovery**: Identifying inhibitors, agonists, or bioactive molecules
    27	
    28	## Installation and Setup
    29	
    30	### Python Client
    31	
    32	The ChEMBL Python client is required for programmatic access:
    33	
    34	```bash
    35	uv pip install chembl_webresource_client
    36	```
    37	
    38	### Basic Usage Pattern
    39	
    40	```python
    41	from chembl_webresource_client.new_client import new_client
    42	
    43	# Access different endpoints
    44	molecule = new_client.molecule
    45	target = new_client.target
    46	activity = new_client.activity
    47	drug = new_client.drug
    48	```
    49	
    50	## Core Capabilities
    51	
    52	### 1. Molecule Queries
    53	
    54	**Retrieve by ChEMBL ID:**
    55	```python
    56	molecule = new_client.molecule
    57	aspirin = molecule.get('CHEMBL25')
    58	```
    59	
    60	**Search by name:**
    61	```python
    62	results = molecule.filter(pref_name__icontains='aspirin')
    63	```
    64	
    65	**Filter by properties:**
    66	```python
    67	# Find small molecules (MW <= 500) with favorable LogP
    68	results = molecule.filter(
    69	    molecule_properties__mw_freebase__lte=500,
    70	    molecule_properties__alogp__lte=5
    71	)
    72	```
    73	
    74	### 2. Target Queries
    75	
    76	**Retrieve target information:**
    77	```python
    78	target = new_client.target
    79	egfr = target.get('CHEMBL203')
    80	```
    81	
    82	**Search for specific target types:**
    83	```python
    84	# Find all kinase targets
    85	kinases = target.filter(
    86	    target_type='SINGLE PROTEIN',
    87	    pref_name__icontains='kinase'
    88	)
    89	```
    90	
    91	### 3. Bioactivity Data
    92	
    93	**Query activities for a target:**
    94	```python
    95	activity = new_client.activity
    96	# Find potent EGFR inhibitors
    97	results = activity.filter(
    98	    target_chembl_id='CHEMBL203',
    99	    standard_type='IC50',
   100	    standard_value__lte=100,
   101	    standard_units='nM'
   102	)
   103	```
   104	
   105	**Get all activities for a compound:**
   106	```python
   107	compound_activities = activity.filter(
   108	    molecule_chembl_id='CHEMBL25',
   109	    pchembl_value__isnull=False
   110	)
   111	```
   112	
   113	### 4. Structure-Based Searches
   114	
   115	**Similarity search:**
   116	```python
   117	similarity = new_client.similarity
   118	# Find compounds similar to aspirin
   119	similar = similarity.filter(
   120	    smiles='CC(=O)Oc1ccccc1C(=O)O',
   121	    similarity=85  # 85% similarity threshold
   122	)
   123	```
   124	
   125	**Substructure search:**
   126	```python
   127	substructure = new_client.substructure
   128	# Find compounds containing benzene ring
   129	results = substructure.filter(smiles='c1ccccc1')
   130	```
   131	
   132	### 5. Drug Information
   133	
   134	**Retrieve drug data:**
   135	```python
   136	drug = new_client.drug
   137	drug_info = drug.get('CHEMBL25')
   138	```
   139	
   140	**Get mechanisms of action:**
   141	```python
   142	mechanism = new_client.mechanism
   143	mechanisms = mechanism.filter(molecule_chembl_id='CHEMBL25')
   144	```
   145	
   146	**Query drug indications:**
   147	```python
   148	drug_indication = new_client.drug_indication
   149	indications = drug_indication.filter(molecule_chembl_id='CHEMBL25')
   150	```
   151	
   152	## Query Workflow
   153	
   154	### Workflow 1: Finding Inhibitors for a Target
   155	
   156	1. **Identify the target** by searching by name:
   157	   ```python
   158	   targets = new_client.target.filter(pref_name__icontains='EGFR')
   159	   target_id = targets[0]['target_chembl_id']
   160	   ```
   161	
   162	2. **Query bioactivity data** for that target:
   163	   ```python
   164	   activities = new_client.activity.filter(
   165	       target_chembl_id=target_id,
   166	       standard_type='IC50',
   167	       standard_value__lte=100
   168	   )
   169	   ```
   170	
   171	3. **Extract compound IDs** and retrieve details:
   172	   ```python
   173	   compound_ids = [act['molecule_chembl_id'] for act in activities]
   174	   compounds = [new_client.molecule.get(cid) for cid in compound_ids]
   175	   ```
   176	
   177	### Workflow 2: Analyzing a Known Drug
   178	
   179	1. **Get drug information**:
   180	   ```python
   181	   drug_info = new_client.drug.get('CHEMBL1234')
   182	   ```
   183	
   184	2. **Retrieve mechanisms**:
   185	   ```python
   186	   mechanisms = new_client.mechanism.filter(molecule_chembl_id='CHEMBL1234')
   187	   ```
   188	
   189	3. **Find all bioactivities**:
   190	   ```python
   191	   activities = new_client.activity.filter(molecule_chembl_id='CHEMBL1234')
   192	   ```
   193	
   194	### Workflow 3: Structure-Activity Relationship (SAR) Study
   195	
   196	1. **Find similar compounds**:
   197	   ```python
   198	   similar = new_client.similarity.filter(smiles='query_smiles', similarity=80)
   199	   ```
   200	
   201	2. **Get activities for each compound**:
   202	   ```python
   203	   for compound in similar:
   204	       activities = new_client.activity.filter(
   205	           molecule_chembl_id=compound['molecule_chembl_id']
   206	       )
   207	   ```
   208	
   209	3. **Analyze property-activity relationships** using molecular properties from results.
   210	
   211	## Filter Operators
   212	
   213	ChEMBL supports Django-style query filters:
   214	
   215	- `__exact` - Exact match
   216	- `__iexact` - Case-insensitive exact match
   217	- `__contains` / `__icontains` - Substring matching
   218	- `__startswith` / `__endswith` - Prefix/suffix matching
   219	- `__gt`, `__gte`, `__lt`, `__lte` - Numeric comparisons
   220	- `__range` - Value in range
   221	- `__in` - Value in list
   222	- `__isnull` - Null/not null check
   223	
   224	## Data Export and Analysis
   225	
   226	Convert results to pandas DataFrame for analysis:
   227	
   228	```python
   229	import pandas as pd
   230	
   231	activities = new_client.activity.filter(target_chembl_id='CHEMBL203')
   232	df = pd.DataFrame(list(activities))
   233	
   234	# Analyze results
   235	print(df['standard_value'].describe())
   236	print(df.groupby('standard_type').size())
   237	```
   238	
   239	## Performance Optimization
   240	
   241	### Caching
   242	
   243	The client automatically caches results for 24 hours. Configure caching:
   244	
   245	```python
   246	from chembl_webresource_client.settings import Settings
   247	
   248	# Disable caching
   249	Settings.Instance().CACHING = False
   250	
   251	# Adjust cache expiration (seconds)
   252	Settings.Instance().CACHE_EXPIRE = 86400
   253	```
   254	
   255	### Lazy Evaluation
   256	
   257	Queries execute only when data is accessed. Convert to list to force execution:
   258	
   259	```python
   260	# Query is not executed yet
   261	results = molecule.filter(pref_name__icontains='aspirin')
   262	
   263	# Force execution
   264	results_list = list(results)
   265	```
   266	
   267	### Pagination
   268	
   269	Results are paginated automatically. Iterate through all results:
   270	
   271	```python
   272	for activity in new_client.activity.filter(target_chembl_id='CHEMBL203'):
   273	    # Process each activity
   274	    print(activity['molecule_chembl_id'])
   275	```
   276	
   277	## Common Use Cases
   278	
   279	### Find Kinase Inhibitors
   280	
   281	```python
   282	# Identify kinase targets
   283	kinases = new_client.target.filter(
   284	    target_type='SINGLE PROTEIN',
   285	    pref_name__icontains='kinase'
   286	)
   287	
   288	# Get potent inhibitors
   289	for kinase in kinases[:5]:  # First 5 kinases
   290	    activities = new_client.activity.filter(
   291	        target_chembl_id=kinase['target_chembl_id'],
   292	        standard_type='IC50',
   293	        standard_value__lte=50
   294	    )
   295	```
   296	
   297	### Explore Drug Repurposing
   298	
   299	```python
   300	# Get approved drugs
   301	drugs = new_client.drug.filter()
   302	
   303	# For each drug, find all targets
   304	for drug in drugs[:10]:
   305	    mechanisms = new_client.mechanism.filter(
   306	        molecule_chembl_id=drug['molecule_chembl_id']
   307	    )
   308	```
   309	
   310	### Virtual Screening
   311	
   312	```python
   313	# Find compounds with desired properties
   314	candidates = new_client.molecule.filter(
   315	    molecule_properties__mw_freebase__range=[300, 500],
   316	    molecule_properties__alogp__lte=5,
   317	    molecule_properties__hba__lte=10,
   318	    molecule_properties__hbd__lte=5
   319	)
   320	```
   321	
   322	## Resources
   323	
   324	### scripts/example_queries.py
   325	
   326	Ready-to-use Python functions demonstrating common ChEMBL query patterns:
   327	
   328	- `get_molecule_info()` - Retrieve molecule details by ID
   329	- `search_molecules_by_name()` - Name-based molecule search
   330	- `find_molecules_by_properties()` - Property-based filtering
   331	- `get_bioactivity_data()` - Query bioactivities for targets
   332	- `find_similar_compounds()` - Similarity searching
   333	- `substructure_search()` - Substructure matching
   334	- `get_drug_info()` - Retrieve drug information
   335	- `find_kinase_inhibitors()` - Specialized kinase inhibitor search
   336	- `export_to_dataframe()` - Convert results to pandas DataFrame
   337	
   338	Consult this script for implementation details and usage examples.
   339	
   340	### references/api_reference.md
   341	
   342	Comprehensive API documentation including:
   343	
   344	- Complete endpoint listing (molecule, target, activity, assay, drug, etc.)
   345	- All filter operators and query patterns
   346	- Molecular properties and bioactivity fields
   347	- Advanced query examples
   348	- Configuration and performance tuning
   349	- Error handling and rate limiting
   350	
   351	Refer to this document when detailed API information is needed or when troubleshooting queries.
   352	
   353	## Important Notes
   354	
   355	### Data Reliability
   356	
   357	- ChEMBL data is manually curated but may contain inconsistencies
   358	- Always check `data_validity_comment` field in activity records
   359	- Be aware of `potential_duplicate` flags
   360	
   361	### Units and Standards
   362	
   363	- Bioactivity values use standard units (nM, uM, etc.)
   364	- `pchembl_value` provides normalized activity (-log scale)
   365	- Check `standard_type` to understand measurement type (IC50, Ki, EC50, etc.)
   366	
   367	### Rate Limiting
   368	
   369	- Respect ChEMBL's fair usage policies
   370	- Use caching to minimize repeated requests
   371	- Consider bulk downloads for large datasets
   372	- Avoid hammering the API with rapid consecutive requests
   373	
   374	### Chemical Structure Formats
   375	
   376	- SMILES strings are the primary structure format
   377	- InChI keys available for compounds
   378	- SVG images can be generated via the image endpoint
   379	
   380	## Additional Resources
   381	
   382	- ChEMBL website: https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chembl/
   383	- API documentation: https://www.ebi.ac.uk/chembl/api/data/docs
   384	- Python client GitHub: https://github.com/chembl/chembl_webresource_client
   385	- Interface documentation: https://chembl.gitbook.io/chembl-interface-documentation/
   386	- Example notebooks: https://github.com/chembl/notebooks
